## Structure: screens, navigation and roles

#### driver role in fleet mode `RI-042`
**OFFICIAL** · role_pattern · impact high · confidence low · flags: checker_same_vendor

Drivers receive a dispatch from a dispatcher and must confirm or reject the assigned load within 30 minutes, or it is booked automatically.

> Once a dispatcher assigns a load, you’ll receive a notification prompting you to confirm the load within 30 minutes.
> uber.com

- Source `S-079`: uber.com (help) https://help.uber.com/en/freight/carrier/article/using-fleet-mode?nodeId=0bcbf15c-b4ff-4c9b-8d29-d2c9514de3bb
- Source `S-107`: mazeconsultancy.com (blog, 2026-10-12) https://mazeconsultancy.com/truck-dispatcher-software
- Source `S-108`: messagedesk.com (blog, 2022-08-12) https://messagedesk.com/blog/sms-text-dispatch-logistics-trucking-transportation
- Source `S-155`: tentrucks.com (blog, 2026-05-14) https://tentrucks.com/dispatching
- Source `S-160`: truckbase.com (blog, 2023-11-29) https://truckbase.com/trucking-dispatch-software
- Source `S-172`: zuzhq.com (blog, 2026-04-16) https://zuzhq.com/en/blog/tms-dispatch-software-guide
- role: driver
- typical_tasks: confirm or reject an assigned load within 30 minutes; book loads in fleet mode when permitted; view load rates when permitted; receive load details; update load status; capture POD documents; communicate with dispatch from phone; capture BOL and POD photos; send real-time status updates; message dispatch; confirm load changes by reply; receive route plans and daily schedules (+7 more)
- typical_permissions: see load prices in the app when the dispatcher allows it; book loads on the app when the dispatcher allows it

#### Auto match several invoices to one freight bill `RI-090`
**OFFICIAL** · workflow · impact high · confidence high · flags: checker_same_vendor, needs-confirm, single-source

When several freight invoices match one freight bill during auto matching, invoices are sorted by amount, largest first, and matched one by one until no positive remaining amount is left on the freight bill.

> All unmatched freight invoices are sorted by amount.
> microsoft.com, 2026-06-22

- Source `S-092`: microsoft.com (help, 2026-06-22) https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/dynamics365/supply-chain/transportation/reconcile-freight-transportation-management
- Seen in 1 of 25 (advanced)
- name: Auto matching of several invoices
- steps: Sort all unmatched freight invoices by amount, largest first; Match the freight invoices one by one to the freight bill; Set the remaining amount depending on the audit master setup and the remaining amount on the freight invoices; Stop when the freight bill is fully matched; later invoices are not processed
- exceptions: Once the freight bill is fully matched, the remaining invoices are not processed
- Applies to: FTR:FTR-011 (refines), ROL:ROL-008 (refines), FLW:FLW-004 (refines)
- Use: Auto matching sorts invoices by amount, largest first, against one freight bill.

#### Match negative invoices manually first `RI-132`
**OFFICIAL** · workflow · impact high · confidence high · flags: checker_same_vendor, needs-confirm, single-source

- Use: Negative freight invoices should be matched manually to control the sequence.

#### Freight reconciliation from carrier invoice to payment `RI-117`
**OFFICIAL** · workflow · impact normal · confidence high · appendix (over_store_limit)

It can start once the carrier invoice arrives, and paper invoices are first turned into electronic ones.

> If it's received on paper, generate an electronic invoice by using the freight bill as a template.

- Seen in 1 of 25 (advanced)
- name: Freight reconciliation end to end
- steps: Receive the freight invoice from the shipping carrier electronically or on paper; If the invoice is on paper, generate an electronic invoice by using the freight bill as a template; Match the freight bills to each freight invoice, manually line by line or by auto matching all invoices at once; Handle any variances between freight bill and invoice with the reconciliation reason codes
- alternates: Reconcile manually line by line; Reconcile automatically by auto matching all available invoices at once
- exceptions: Variances between freight bill and invoice are handled with reconciliation reason codes set up for manual and automatic reconciliation
- Applies to: FTR:FTR-011 (refines), ROL:ROL-008 (refines), FLW:FLW-004 (refines)
- Use: Usual journey: use it for steps, alternatives and exceptions.

- role: operations team
- steps: Automated tracking pings the carrier every 15 minutes for a carrier PRO Number while Dispatched; Shipment advances to In Transit automatically once the carrier PRO Number is received; Automated tracking checks for a status update every 4 hours while In Transit; Shipment is set to Delivered automatically when the status update is received
- alternates: Without automated tracking, Dispatched shipments are reviewed with the carrier's dispatch team to confirm receipt
- exceptions: If a shipment is canceled on the TMS after dispatch via API or EDI, the dispatch request to the carrier is NOT canceled and the carrier must be contacted
- Applies to: FTR:FTR-001 (refines), ROL:ROL-001 (refines), FLW:FLW-003 (refines)

- alternates: Dispatchers can manage everything from the browser or on the go, using a mobile view to monitor progress.; Drivers use a dedicated phone app for turn-by-turn navigation, status updates and proof of delivery.
